    ELSE N'Unknown Recipe'

    WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NULL THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)

    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END, [u].[UomName], [m].[MaterialID], [u].[UomID], [p0].[BatchID], [p0].[BatchNumber], [p0].[Status], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [b].[ResultStatus]
    FROM [BatchProcessLogs] AS [b]
    WHERE [p0].[BatchID] = [b].[BatchID]
    ORDER BY [b].[LogID] DESC), [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialId], [t].[RequiredQuantity], [t].[DispensingStatus], [t].[MaterialName], [t].[MaterialCode], [t].[UomName], [t].[MaterialID0], [t].[UomID], CASE
    WHEN NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p2]
        WHERE [p].[OrderID] = [p2].[OrderId] AND [p2].[DispensingStatus] <> N'Dispensed') THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END
FROM [ProductionOrders] AS [p]

LEFT JOIN [UnitOfMeasure] AS [u] ON [m].[BaseUomID] = [u].[UomID]
LEFT JOIN [ProductionBatches] AS [p0] ON [p].[OrderID] = [p0].[OrderID]

        WHEN [m0].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m0].[MaterialName]
        ELSE N'Unknown'
    END AS [MaterialName], CASE
        WHEN [m0].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m0].[MaterialCode]
        ELSE ''

        ELSE N'N/A'
    END AS [UomName], [m0].[MaterialID] AS [MaterialID0], [u0].[UomID], [p1].[OrderId]
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p1]
    LEFT JOIN [Materials] AS [m0] ON [p1].[MaterialId] = [m0].[MaterialID]
    LEFT JOIN [UnitOfMeasure] AS [u0] ON [p1].[UomId] = [u0].[UomID]
) AS [t] ON [p].[OrderID] = [t].[OrderId]

    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)

    SELECT TOP(1) [b].[ResultStatus]
    FROM [BatchProcessLogs] AS [b]
    WHERE [p0].[BatchID] = [b].[BatchID]
    ORDER BY [b].[LogID] DESC), [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialId], [t].[RequiredQuantity], [t].[DispensingStatus], [t].[MaterialName], [t].[MaterialCode], [t].[UomName], [t].[MaterialID0], [t].[UomID], CASE

                    WHEN [u1].[UomID] IS NOT NULL THEN [u1].[UomName]
                    ELSE N'N/A'
                END AS [UomName0]
                FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p2]
                LEFT JOIN [Materials] AS [m1] ON [p2].[MaterialId] = [m1].[MaterialID]
                LEFT JOIN [UnitOfMeasure] AS [u1] ON [p2].[UomId] = [u1].[UomID]
                WHERE [p].[OrderID] = [p2].[OrderId]
            ) AS [t1]
            WHERE ([t].[MaterialId] = [t1].[MaterialId] OR ([t].[MaterialId] IS NULL AND [t1].[MaterialId] IS NULL)) AND ([t].[MaterialName0] = [t1].[MaterialName0] OR ([t].[MaterialName0] IS NULL AND [t1].[MaterialName0] IS NULL)) AND ([t].[MaterialCode0] = [t1].[MaterialCode0] OR ([t].[MaterialCode0] IS NULL AND [t1].[MaterialCode0] IS NULL)) AND ([t].[UomName0] = [t1].[UomName0] OR ([t].[UomName0] IS NULL AND [t1].[UomName0] IS NULL)))
        ELSE 0
    END AS [OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialId], COALESCE(SUM([t].[RequiredQuantity]), 0.0) AS [RequiredQuantity], CASE
        WHEN NOT EXISTS (

                    ELSE ''

            ) AS [t2]
            WHERE ([t].[MaterialId] = [t2].[MaterialId] OR ([t].[MaterialId] IS NULL AND [t2].[MaterialId] IS NULL)) AND ([t].[MaterialName0] = [t2].[MaterialName0] OR ([t].[MaterialName0] IS NULL AND [t2].[MaterialName0] IS NULL)) AND ([t].[MaterialCode0] = [t2].[MaterialCode0] OR ([t].[MaterialCode0] IS NULL AND [t2].[MaterialCode0] IS NULL)) AND ([t].[UomName0] = [t2].[UomName0] OR ([t].[UomName0] IS NULL AND [t2].[UomName0] IS NULL)) AND [t2].[DispensingStatus] <> N'Dispensed') THEN N'Dispensed'
        ELSE N'Pending'
    END AS [DispensingStatus], [t].[MaterialName0] AS [MaterialName], [t].[MaterialCode0] AS [MaterialCode], [t].[UomName0] AS [UomName]
    FROM (
        SELECT [p4].[MaterialId], [p4].[RequiredQuantity], CASE

        WHERE [p].[OrderID] = [p4].[OrderId]
    ) AS [t]

) AS [t3]

SELECT [p].[BatchID], [p].[BatchNumber], [p].[OrderID], CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL THEN [p1].[OrderCode]
    ELSE ''
END, CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL AND [r].[RecipeID] IS NOT NULL THEN [r].[RecipeName]
    ELSE N'Sản phẩm'
END, [p].[Status], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialId], [t].[RequiredQuantity], [t].[DispensingStatus], [t].[MaterialName], [t].[MaterialCode], [t].[UomName], [t].[MaterialID0], [t].[UomID], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p3]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p3].[BatchId]) AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p4]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p4].[BatchId] AND [p4].[DispensingStatus] <> N'Dispensed') THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END
FROM [ProductionBatches] AS [p]
LEFT JOIN [ProductionOrders] AS [p1] ON [p].[OrderID] = [p1].[OrderID]
LEFT JOIN [Recipes] AS [r] ON [p1].[RecipeID] = [r].[RecipeID]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p2].[OrderBomId], [p2].[MaterialId], [p2].[RequiredQuantity], [p2].[DispensingStatus], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialName]
        ELSE N'Unknown'
    END AS [MaterialName], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialCode]
        ELSE ''
    END AS [MaterialCode], CASE
        WHEN [u].[UomID] IS NOT NULL THEN [u].[UomName]
        ELSE N'N/A'
    END AS [UomName], [m].[MaterialID] AS [MaterialID0], [u].[UomID], [p2].[BatchId]
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p2]
    LEFT JOIN [Materials] AS [m] ON [p2].[MaterialId] = [m].[MaterialID]
    LEFT JOIN [UnitOfMeasure] AS [u] ON [p2].[UomId] = [u].[UomID]
) AS [t] ON [p].[BatchID] = [t].[BatchId]
WHERE [p].[Status] IN (N'Scheduled', N'In-Process') AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p0]
    WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p0].[BatchId])
ORDER BY [p].[BatchID], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialID0]

SELECT [p].[BatchID], [p].[BatchNumber], [p].[OrderID], CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL THEN [p1].[OrderCode]
    ELSE ''
END, CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL AND [r].[RecipeID] IS NOT NULL THEN [r].[RecipeName]
    ELSE N'Sản phẩm'
END, [p].[Status], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialId], [t].[RequiredQuantity], [t].[DispensingStatus], [t].[MaterialName], [t].[MaterialCode], [t].[UomName], [t].[MaterialID0], [t].[UomID], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p3]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p3].[BatchId]) AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p4]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p4].[BatchId] AND [p4].[DispensingStatus] <> N'Dispensed') THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END
FROM [ProductionBatches] AS [p]
LEFT JOIN [ProductionOrders] AS [p1] ON [p].[OrderID] = [p1].[OrderID]
LEFT JOIN [Recipes] AS [r] ON [p1].[RecipeID] = [r].[RecipeID]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p2].[OrderBomId], [p2].[MaterialId], [p2].[RequiredQuantity], [p2].[DispensingStatus], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialName]
        ELSE N'Unknown'
    END AS [MaterialName], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialCode]
        ELSE ''
    END AS [MaterialCode], CASE
        WHEN [u].[UomID] IS NOT NULL THEN [u].[UomName]
        ELSE N'N/A'
    END AS [UomName], [m].[MaterialID] AS [MaterialID0], [u].[UomID], [p2].[BatchId]
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p2]
    LEFT JOIN [Materials] AS [m] ON [p2].[MaterialId] = [m].[MaterialID]
    LEFT JOIN [UnitOfMeasure] AS [u] ON [p2].[UomId] = [u].[UomID]
) AS [t] ON [p].[BatchID] = [t].[BatchId]
WHERE [p].[Status] IN (N'Scheduled', N'In-Process') AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p0]
    WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p0].[BatchId])
ORDER BY [p].[BatchID], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialID0]

SELECT [p].[BatchID], [p].[BatchNumber], [p].[OrderID], CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL THEN [p1].[OrderCode]
    ELSE ''
END, CASE
    WHEN [p1].[OrderID] IS NOT NULL AND [r].[RecipeID] IS NOT NULL THEN [r].[RecipeName]
    ELSE N'Sản phẩm'
END, [p].[Status], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialId], [t].[RequiredQuantity], [t].[DispensingStatus], [t].[MaterialName], [t].[MaterialCode], [t].[UomName], [t].[MaterialID0], [t].[UomID], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p3]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p3].[BatchId]) AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p4]
        WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p4].[BatchId] AND [p4].[DispensingStatus] <> N'Dispensed') THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END
FROM [ProductionBatches] AS [p]
LEFT JOIN [ProductionOrders] AS [p1] ON [p].[OrderID] = [p1].[OrderID]
LEFT JOIN [Recipes] AS [r] ON [p1].[RecipeID] = [r].[RecipeID]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p2].[OrderBomId], [p2].[MaterialId], [p2].[RequiredQuantity], [p2].[DispensingStatus], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialName]
        ELSE N'Unknown'
    END AS [MaterialName], CASE
        WHEN [m].[MaterialID] IS NOT NULL THEN [m].[MaterialCode]
        ELSE ''
    END AS [MaterialCode], CASE
        WHEN [u].[UomID] IS NOT NULL THEN [u].[UomName]
        ELSE N'N/A'
    END AS [UomName], [m].[MaterialID] AS [MaterialID0], [u].[UomID], [p2].[BatchId]
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p2]
    LEFT JOIN [Materials] AS [m] ON [p2].[MaterialId] = [m].[MaterialID]
    LEFT JOIN [UnitOfMeasure] AS [u] ON [p2].[UomId] = [u].[UomID]
) AS [t] ON [p].[BatchID] = [t].[BatchId]
WHERE [p].[Status] IN (N'Scheduled', N'In-Process') AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [ProductionOrderBom] AS [p0]
    WHERE [p].[BatchID] = [p0].[BatchId])
ORDER BY [p].[BatchID], [p1].[OrderID], [r].[RecipeID], [t].[OrderBomId], [t].[MaterialID0]
